2a1(ya): ba/dkar ser dmar ldang mthong ga' ye/gru gsum 'bar ba drag po'i gzhal yas khang na
2a2(ya): gsapa 'cher lhun sdug gar byin 'tho ga khrag 'thung rig pa'i he ru ka la kro d+hi sha'i rig pa'i yum dang 'brel ci
2a3(ya): mal gro rgyam tsho tshogs dang lenas gyun grub tu mas cang pa/'gas 'azugs pa
2b1(ya): 'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/khrag 'thung rig pa'i rtsal phyir 'khyam pas ma rigs bdag 'dzin gyi ru tra lngar gyur te/gshin sa
2b2(ya): dang /gshin chu dang /gshin me dang /gshin rlung dang /gashrin e nagA lnga dang /de rnams kyi chung ma srid pa sha za/srid pa khrag 'thung /srid pa drod
2b3(ya): 'phrog dang /srid pa dbugs len/srid pa'i rgyal mo mu kha le dang lngas khams gsum kun gyis srog gcod cing /sprul pa dang yang sprul sna tshogs
2b4(ya): par skye ste/'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug dang /nga rgyal phrag dog srung bskyed nas/sa chu me rlung na mkha' la gnas bcas te sems can gyis
3a1(ya): snying la zhugs nas/'gro ba sdug bsngal gyi nyam thag pa de'i tshe de'i dus na khrag 'thung rig pa'i he ru ka lngas/thugs rje rang byung gi 'od
3a2(ya): zer steng 'og phyogs mtshams med par 'phroso//ma rig bdag 'dzin 'joms pa'i mtshon chas kheng sa/drag po sngags kyis rang sgra drag la che//
3a3(ya): 'jigs byed khro gtum gyi sku ni che la mang bas ma rig bdag 'dzin gyi ru tra lnga/srid pa srog 'phrog kyi chung ma lnga dang /sprul pa dang yang spyil sna tshogs/
3a4(ya): pa rnams stong gsal 'dzin med du brgyal te ye ji bzhin par brgyal bar sangs nas khrag 'thung yab yum lnga rang du sheso//oM sku'i dbang bskur bas
3a5(ya): gshin rje chung ma dang bcas pa/khrag 'thung lha yab yum du bsgyur te/'jigs byed phyag bzhi pa yab yum gyis bstan pa'i mgon byed par khas blangs
3b1(ya): A gsung gi dbang bskur bas zhi rgyas dbang drag bzhi ru bsgyur te 'jigs byed chen po'i 'phrin las kyi bsgrub pa po mgon byed par khas blangaso//hUM thugs kyi dbang bskur bas/skye med 'od gsal 'dzin du sangs
3b2(ya): rgyas te/sems can gyi snying la gnas bcas pas/sen kun gyi mgon byed par khas blangaso//sku brjod las 'das pas na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam pa'o//gsung brjod las 'das pas 'phrin las bzhi la
3b3(ya): 'dus pa'o//thugs brjod las 'das pas bsgral tshang byang chub las so//mtshan yang yis mgon po phyag bzhi pa zhes grag skrod//grag go grags par gyur pas nag po'i phyogs kyi ma mo dang /lha dang klu dang /bdud dang
3b4(ya): gza' dang /tshangs pa dang /gshin rje dang /btsan dang /gnod sbyin dang /mi srun pa du ma lhags te/da ni sangs rgyas 'byung ngo //'dis bstan pa bsrung bar 'gyur ro//da ni chos dar ro//ha la ha la ces mi dga'i
3b5(ya): tshig tu ma smra zhing /nang rigs du ma'i rlung yang 'tshub so//der khas blangs rje su dran nas/dregs pa zil gnon gyi ting 'dzin las bzhengs te/oM AHhUM klo d+ha mA ra na zhes pa'i sgra sgrags pas/shar du sa
3b6(ya): g.yos/lho ru glog 'khrugs/nub tu 'brug ldir byang du thog babs/sku khros pas zhal gyi dkyi lor gzugs med kyi khams nas khro zhing gtsigs/sku lus gzugs khams ni 'khyil ba phyag bzhin ni/
4a1(ya): @#/dang ral gri dang /zong khrag dang rtse gsum bsnams pa/zhabs gnyis phyed skyil du bzhugs te/pad+ma nyi zla'i gdan la 'dod khams rigs drug mnan nas/khro bo dang khyung gis 'jig rten gyi khams bskang ngo //gsung
4a2(ya): khros pas/oM AHhUM ma hA ka la e ka dza te ma ma hUM hUM//dgra bgegs mA ra ya mA ra ya hUM hUM phaT phaT/zhe stong gsum du bsgragaso//thugs khrod pas gzi brjid bskal pa'i me ltar 'bar te/log pa'i dgra bgegs zhe la bcangs nas skye
4a3(ya): med chos sku'i zhing du sgrol bar dam bcas pas/mi bsrun pa tha~Md brgya la rengs khrag tu skyugaso//dkar po'i phyogs kyi lha dang mi dang lha ma yin dang /dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yi rangs te mchod pa'i sprin btibs shing bstod pas hUM
4a4(ya): riri ta raM dmar nag me'i dkyil 'khor nas/pad+ma nyi zla ro stengasu//dpa ldan yas mgon po ni/sku mdog mthing nag gsus po che/zhal cig phyag bzhi phyed dkyil bzhugs//g.yas gnyis tsidz+dza ral gri bsnams/g.yon gnyis//
4a5(ya): zod khrag kha T+wAM bsnams//zhal gdangs ljags 'dril mche ba gtsigs//khro gnyer spyan gsum dmar la 'khrug/ag tshom smin ma dbu skra ser/thod skam rin chen sbrul gyis brgyan//mi mgo'i ro shal stag lpag
4a6(ya): dkris//'jigs byed chen po phyag 'tshal bstod//ces dpal nag po chen po'i sku gsung thugs pa'i rgyung /'thung 'khrul rang shes gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o///de nas 'og min ban+d+ha 'bar ba'i gzhal yas skam rlon gnyid pa bachi//
4a7(ya): bad khrag dang zhag tu 'khyil ba zhing lpags kyi bla re la zhing chung 'phan 'chang ba/rag ta'i mtsho la tsit+ta'i me tog bkram pa/e ka dza kar mo dmar mo dang /ljang khu nag mo 'bum gyi gtso'i mgo//bod mkhar rgyu'i bdag mo/srid pa las kyi rgyal ma
4b1(ya): dang /srid pa las kyi 'byung khungs ma zhes bya ba'i bu mo dze ti dbang phyug ma zhes bya bas rus pa'i rgyal gsolo//rag+ha dmar gyi skyems drangs nas/phyag dang bskor ba byas shing yis mgon po nyid la 'khril te/dgyes chen su ka ma hA a mri ta/e ma do
4b2(ya): mtshar rab 'jig so//ma rungs dregs pa'i tshogs rnams la/zhing bcu sgrol ba'i las bcol gyis/thugs rjes dgongs nas brgyal rengs slong /khrag skyugs 'phrin las dag la dgos/ces ni zhu ba rab zhus pas/'jigs byed yis mgon po
4b3(ya): yis//rum khro dgyes pa'i 'dzum snyan nas/d+harma kA ya shan+ti ku/zhes pa'i sgra yis brgyal bslangs nas/rang rang phyogs kyi sa le 'khod/de nas seng gdong gis gus pa'i tshul gyis 'di skad gsol//hUM gtso mchog 'bar ba'i phyag bzhi pa/
4b4(ya): mthing nag rab 'jigs yis mgon/dregs pa'i tshogs bdag skyabasu mchi/brgyal rengs khrag skyug grol mdzad cing /kun la drin che lha mo'i sku/yum mchog ce'u ri dbang phyug gtso//sku mdog nag mo khro tshul can//zhal cig phyaganyis
4b5(ya): thod skam brgyan/phyag yas gri gug dgra snying 'byin/phyag yon thong khrag tso la stobs/mche gcig khri gnyer sman gsum dmar/gtsur skra grol rus pas brgyan//zhabs gnyis yab la 'khril cing sbyor/gnyis med bde chen he ru ka/khrag 'thung
4b6(ya): yab yum phyag 'tshal stod/che mchog nyid kyis spyan snga ru/khas blangs khrag 'thung yab yum gyis/bka' nyan las byed srog 'bul lo//si na ru hUM phaT tri hri da dza/bskrad pa'i gtor ma dag la zhu//gar rbang rlag byed bsgrub mchog skyong /sa
4b7(ya): ma yA srin po ha la ba ri dang /da ki sha za nag mo'i bu/sha ba khrag 'thung byi ros gdong /gus pa'i tshul gyis 'di skad gsol/che mchog sprul pa khrag 'bring rgyal/dregs pa'i tshogs la bdag skyabasu mchi//ni rgyas dbang drag 'drin las sgo//dkar
5a1(ya): @/ser dmar ljang mthing ka yis/las grub yab yum phyag 'tshal bstod//bstan bsrung gtso bo yab yum gyis/bka' nyan las byed srog 'bru'o//ka kha mu kha hUMs ma ya srog drung dza dza/sha chang dmar gyi gtor ma nu/bstan pa bsrub
5a2(ya): zhing bsgrub mchog skyong //srin mo smag 'bar mi thod can//bdud po kos rje'i bu mo ni/'bar ma nag mos 'di skad gsol/che mchog sprul pa phyag bzhi pa/bka' nyan las byed srog 'bu'o//ma ma nag mo dz+wa li ru tri dza dza/rag+hA sha khrag gtor ma zhu//
5a3(ya): bla tshe phrag nas bsgrub mchog skyong /klu mo klog lcam 'bar ma dang /srin po rak+sha ha li'i bu/ra hu la yi 'di skad gsol/chos sku 'gyur med nag mo che/'bris lhun grub phyag bzhi'o//gnyis med bde chen yab yum gyis/bka' nyan las bgyid srog
5a4(ya): 'bulo//rag ra hu la ba hring dza/sha khrag 'di la gtor ma zhu/bla srog 'byed cing bsgrub mchog skyong /klu chen brgyad kyis 'di skad gsol/khrag 'thung khyung gi tshogs rnams lha/gus 'dud bka' nyan rang srog 'bul/nA ga nA ga ra dza bung hring dza/dug
5a5(ya): rlangs gtong zhing bsgrub mchog skyong /shar phyogs rgyal pos 'di skad gsol/bdag ni khrag 'thung bka' la nyan/rang srog 'bul zhing dam nyams sgrol//ging kar ra ra hring dza/bshos bu gser skyems gtor ma zhu/smyo 'bog gtong zhing bsgrub mchog skyong
5a6(ya): sa ma ya nA ra ka na/lho phyogs gshin rjes 'di skad gsol//bdag ni khrag 'thung bka' la nyan/rang srog 'bul zhing dam nyams sgrol//ya ma rA dza hring dza/snying sna dag gis gtor ma zhu/srog rtsa gcod cing bsgrub mchog skyong /nub phyogs ba tsan gyis
5a7(ya): 'di skad gsel/bdag ni khrag 'thung bkar nyan/rang srog 'bul zhing dam nyams sgrol/badz+ras du ba rdzing dza/ba shes bu sha khrag gtir ma nu//gzer thabs gtong zhing bsgrub mchog smang /khyang phyogs gnod sbyin 'di nad gsil/bdag ni khrag 'byung ba/
5b1(ya): /kha nyan data/rang srog 'bul zhing dam nyams sgrol/yak+Sha nag po dun hring dza/bya dmar sha khrag gtor ma zhu//tshe srog sdud cing sgrub mchog skyong /sa ma ya nA ra ke na/dpal nag po chen po sku gsung thugs khros pa'i rgyud las/srog 'bul khas blangs kyi le'u ste/
5b2(ya): gnyis pa'o///dzA ti nyid kyis yang gsol ba/tshe srog sprul pa dregs pa'i gtso//me srin smin byed de badzin gang //dam tshig bsrung tshul ji ltar bgyi//khyed ni 'dul byed lha ni tsi/spyi bsgrub gnyen pa ji ltar bgyi/sku gsung thugs kyi la
5b3(ya): mkhan dpung /mnyes shing 'du ba'i bsgrub rdzas rnams/'dus pa'i 'khor lo gsung du gsol/nag po chen pos bka' stsal pa/kyai 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs nyon/rin chen bdud rtsis bdag la//dkyil 'khor mda' gang gru bzhi bya/chos 'byung nyis brtsegs
5b4(ya): pad+mad 'dab bzhi//de'i phyi rim 'khor lo bzhi//yang na pad+ma 'dab brgyad bya//bod 'phreng rdo ra bar bcas/me ri dmar nag skor ba la//pad+ma 'khor lo phyogs kyis mtshon/gtor tshogs sum thod sin d+ha ra/mchod lnga gsur chen rtag spong gling /legs par rta gon cho/
5b5(ya): bcas te/spyan drangs bzhug sol mchod gtor dbul/zhi khro gzhung bsrang dbang bskuro//ces snang srid drag po dur khrod brgyad dang bcas pa'i dkyi lor brgyad dang bcas pa'i 'khor du gyur te//tsa ri dbang phyug gis yang gsol pa/dpal ldan 'jigs byed chen po
5b6(ya): kyerta 'jigs pa'i dkyi lor 'di ru/dregs pa pho mo slar zhugs pas/dpa'o bla mas gsung du gsol//dpa'o chen pos bka' stsal pa/to dpa ldan khrag 'thung yab yum gyis//'jigs pa'i dkyi lor 'dir bzhugs legs//sangs bstan pa bsrung ba'i
5b7(ya): byir te drag po 'phrin las grub par mdzad//oM ma hA kA la e ka dza ti/a be sha ya ma ma hUM hUM/yes pags nas ldeg pa dang /ri rab 'gule zhing rgya mtsho 'khyoms//sa rnams g.yo zhing byin phebs pas//sangs zhing khams mtha' yas na/sgrub
6a1(ya): @byed kun gyis rtags thon na/de nas bum pa rab thogs te/nga'i bka' nyan dregs pa'i tshogs//'jigs byed dkyi lor 'di la ltos/bdag ni yab yum he ru ka/'jigs pa'i nga ro ma 'dod 'bar/dpal dang ldan pa'i dbang bskur/
6a2(ya): oM badz+ra rat+na pad+ma karma bud+d+ha/ma hA ka la e ka dza ti a b+hi'iny+tsa a/dbang thob sang+yas kun zhal mthong /bka' nyan bstan bsrung khas drang so//phyag yon thod pa rab gta do//byang sems dkar dmar her lnga'i bcud/bdud rtsi lnga ni khrag 'byung rdzas/dngos
6a3(ya): /grub myur thob dbang bskuro//ma hA ka la e ka dza ti/ma hA su ka a mr-i ti/a b+hi Shiny+tsa a/dbang thob sngags dang dam tshig spyod/sngags kyi sgor zhugs bu ltar dgongs/me long sin+d+hu ra bstan te/dri med shel skong rang rig sems/snang grags me lod
6a4(ya): gzugs brnyan la/yab yum gnyis med sbyor cing rol/gsal la ngo stong dbang bsku ro//oM AHhUM ma hA kA la e ka dza ti/pradz+nyA dz+nyA na a b+hi'iny+tsa hUM/dbang thob mchogs la gnas/chos byed thugs mthong tsam gyis grol/e ma ltos cig ltos/
6a5(ya): cig sems la ltos/lus ni gsal stong nga'i sku/ngag ni grag stong nga'i gsung /sems ni rig steng de'i thugs/ngo bo mthong na ngo bo med/rang bzhin mthong na sangs yin//thugs rje drag po'i las byas na/zhing bcu bar ba'i sa la bkod//khrag 'thung rig/
6a6(ya): ba'i he ru kas/nga yang de ltar smin par byas/sku gsung thugs khrod pa'i rgyud las dbang gi le'u ste/gsum pa'o//dza ti dbang phyug yang gsol pa/dbang gi rim pa de ltar na/dam tshig bsrung tshul blang du gsol/khrag 'thung chen
6a7(ya): bos bka' stsal pa/spyi ru dam tshig 'bum stor te/ya na lag grangs ni dam tshig dang /bsgrub pa'i dam tshig nyi shu bsrungs//rtsa ba sku gsung thugs yin//bla ma yi dam rang re dang /mchod lham sems can ma khrel bsrung //bla dang nor dang
6b1(ya): sku gsung brags/yan lag grangs kyis ma nyams na/dam can mgo bas mthu rtsal 'byung /mang pos bskur bas grags pa ste/yid du'ang zhing sangs rgya'o//dam tshig nyams par gyur pa na/rdo dmyal ba chen por skye/mi bzed sdig bsngal myong bar
6b2(ya): 'gyur sa dam tshig nyams pa'i snga lta su//rims dang nad yams 'tshe ba 'byung /dbang po 'gags cing mdze shu dang /yid du mi 'ong sna tshogs pas/dam tshig nyams pa brlag par byed/des na phyag dang bskor ba dang /sngags bshags ti nge 'dzin dag la
6b3(ya): 'bad //dam ldan bsnyen bsgrub rem pa ni//sprin ltar 'du zhing bu ltar brtse/tsaM bcol 'phrin las kun 'gru'o//sku gsung thugs khros pa'i rgyud las/dam tshig le'u ste bzhi pa'o//dza ti dbang phyug yang gsol pa/dam tshig rim+b+ha pa de ltar
6b4(ya): ni//'dul byed lha ni gsung du gsol//khrag 'thung chen pos bka' stsal pa/nga'i 'dul byed lha nyid ni//khrag 'thung rig pa'i ha ruH/ma bskyed dbyings nas/gsal bar bzhugs//'bral ba med cing gnas pa med//rang gi sems nyid rtogs par gyis/
6b5(ya): nga la skye ba'i yul med pas/'chi ba zhes bya mi 'ong ngo //nga la 'gro dang 'ong med pas/'du dang 'bral zhes ming nas med/ngal gsal dang 'grib pa med/nga la dgyes dang mi dgyes med/nga la sgrub pa'i rdzas kyang med/zlas pa bya ba'i sngags kyang med/thang
6b6(ya): rang gis byed pa yin/me long gzugs brnyan ltar shes na/snang grags 'dul byed lha yino//brag cha lta bu shes pa yin//tshig rnams 'dul byed lha yin//rig pa'i don nyid rtogs pa na/zod 'dul byed lha yino//zhi khro bskyed rim gsal ba na/nga ni 'dul
6b7(ya): byed lha yin//nga nyid yab yum gsal ba na//dregs pa 'dul byed lha yino//khrag 'thung rigs lnga'i snying po ni/nga nyid 'dul zhing zad bsgrags//mchog dang thun mongs dngos grub 'thung //sku gsung thugs khros pa'i rgyud las 'dul byed lha'i le'u stirma/
7a1(ya): pa'o//dza ti nyid kyis yang gsol pa/'dul byed lha ni de ltar na/s+m+R+YuM bsgrub rim pa gsung du gsol/phyag bzhi pa yis bka' stsal pa/dkyil 'khor dbang dang mthun pa lags/phyogs tshon sgo dang bcas pa b+h+ri dbus//
7a2(ya): zod pa kha sbyar nang /gr-I snying bya rog nas rnying dang /'khor lor bcas pa dang nag hU~M ga/nas kyi chom bu gtor mar gcas//yang yum gtso 'khor nyid yin/he ru ka'i da rgyal gyis/snying khar sreg phral sngags bsams te/'ad zer dmar pos dang
7a3(ya): sdus la/gsal ba'i sems kyi 'bum du bskyal//oM ma hA kA la tsa ti gu na tri ta hUM phaT/ma hA kA la ka la mu kha hUM/sa ma ya srog dung dung dzaM dza/ma ma rag mo dz+wa li ru tring dza/sa nar hu hr-ing hring dza/rag ra hu la hring dza/ging ka ra hring dza/ya ma/
7a4(ya): ra dza hri dza/badz+ra sa du hring dza/yag+ma nag po hr-i dza/mi tshor bzlas na snang srid g.yo//'gro ba kun gyi srog 'bralo//ci bsam 'grub cing bstan pa bsrung /sku gsung thugs khros pa'i rgyud las/spyi sgrub kyi le'u ste drug pa'o//
7a5(ya): dza ti nyid kyis yang gsol pa/spyi bsgrub rim pa de ltar na/sku gsung thugs kyi las mkhan gsung /nag po chen po bka' stsal pa/che mchog khros pa'i nus mthu yis/ru tra lnga gtum lnga ru 'gyur//chung ma lnga ni btul ba yis/gtum pa'i dbang phyug
7a6(ya): tsan ti ka/rlon par ral can sha za ma/yo nas lhun grub dgongs pa shes/bstan bsgrub sen mgon du bsgyur//dregs pa 'dul phyir sku che'o//'phris sgrub phyir gsung chen po//sang+yas zhing sbyor thugs che'o//yis mgon po yab yum grags/nga nyid stong
7a7(ya): banyid yang sa ye// byed pas la zer snyun nyid mug/nga nyid pas kya di chen 'dri 'o//sru'i las la pani rig gang /dka' dang bstan pa khyed kyi 'grong /gsung gi las mo khan la y-i nas/bka' dang bstan pa khyod kyis bcug/zungs kyilas ma
7b1(ya): nar phug bka' dang bstan pa khyod kyis bsrungs//chos byed bsgrub mchog dam pa yis/bos pa'i dus na snyan ma sra/g.yab pa'i tshen spyan ma brtul//lus dang grib ma bzhin du 'grogs//rten pa'i chen phrag mdog/rbad pa'i chen mthu ma rung /gcod/
7b2(ya): pa'i tshe na dang ma ring //tsit+ta gtor ma ltag slong rnga nag gling bu gsur chen rung /sha lnga bdud rtsi lnga tshogs na/sha la sbrang bzhin khyed rnams 'du/chos byed dam tshig can la ni/ci yang med las dam la 'du/sngags pa dam tshig mi bsrung na/bsgrub
7b3(ya): kyang bskal nyams las ma byed//bya rog 'bar me seng+ge gdong /bza' bdud rgyal po gshin rje can/gnod sbyin klu dang mi bsrun tshe gre/sangs bstan pa'i bka' 'di bsrungs//rna lhor yongs kyi dgra lha mdzod/rigs 'dzin kun gyi srog shing gyis//dam
7b4(ya): nyams kun gyis srog rtsa chod//bdud 'dul rnams kyi dbang phyug che/gtsug lag khang gi bsrung ma rnams//legs nyes kun gyis stang 'dzin che/nag po stong gi tshogs kyi bskor//ma mo bye ba'i tshogs kyis bskor//sha za 'bum gyi tshogs kyi skor//
7b5(ya): gnod sbyin du ma'i tshigs kyis bskor/bdud pho nyi khri'i tshogs kyis bskor//mi bsrun drag po mthu chen rnams/bstan 'jigs dgra bo zasu gtang /dam nyams sha za khrag ni 'phungs//snying phyung srog tshong dus mtha' bsdus//nor phrogs grong 'gyel nye dang phe/
7b6(ya): lo zlar ma bzhag za mar sbrel//ces yab yum chos nyid mnyam pA'i ting 'dzin la bzhugs te/khrag 'bung rigs pa'i he ru ka la thim//dpal nag po chen po sku gsung thugs khros pa'i rgyud las/las mkhan gnyer gtad pa'i le'u ste bdun
7b7(ya): tham da /de nas lha dang mi dang lha ma yin dang /slang srid kyi lha srin sde brgyad rnams kyis/ges pa'i sha dang bcos pa'i chang /gtsang sgeg la bum gros rigs dang /kha me bu gu shu btsa li dang /rgun 'brum kar bu ram dang /k+ta la sakra
8a1(ya): @#/khrag sna stong /maM sa la sogs sha sna stong /su ra la sogs khrag sna stong /ban d+ha nang khrol rus pa'i tshogs//dar nag rgyal mtshan gsur chen spos//rol mo'i tshigs kyi rab mchod nas/'jigs byed chen po khrag 'thung gA
8a2(ya): gnyer gtad bka' sgo spun gsum la/'grin cig sdebs nas 'di skad gsol//chos dbyings skye med dang shed nas/thabs dang shes rab gdugs pa 'dul/drag po 'jigs byed skur bstan pa'i//mgon po yab yum phyag 'tshal stong//sngon che
8a3(ya): khrag 'thung spyan mnga' ru/bstan pa bsrung bar khas blangs pa'i//srin po'i gzugs 'chang gdug pa 'dul/bya rog gdong la phyag 'tshal bstod//nag po gtsigs mdzad mkha'agro'i gtso//srin po'i gzugs kyi dug char 'bebs/sha za khrag
8a4(ya): 'thung rab 'byams ma/nag mo bya zhon ma la phyag 'tshal bstod//'jig rten las grub dregs pa can/rang sngags smod pa'i mtshon cha yis//srog dbugs 'brel pa'i mthu bo che/rA hu la la phyag 'tshal bstod//de skad bstod cing 'di skad gsol
8a5(ya): dus ni bskal pa phyi ma'i dus kyis tshe/chos la dgra dang 'tshe ba byed pa yis/gtsug lag chos 'khor gnyan po brlag par byed/phyi rabs sngags pa nang dme byas pa yis/zhing bcu gdug pa ru tra du mar 'gyur/de tshe bsgrol ba'i las bsgos ji
8a6(ya): ji ltar 'tshal//bsgrub gnas zla ba gang la bya//dkyil 'khor bslab rdzas ci dang ci//bod cad gsad pa'i las mtha' gang //rtags dang mtshan ma ji ltar 'ong //ma grub spogs cho ga ji ltar bgyi//'phrin+da las bzhi dang las tshogs dang /dang

8b1(ya): gnyis gri gug thod khrag bsnams/stag lpags mi mgo ga yang gzhi gsol//ka kha mu kha kro d+ha mA ra ya/bya rog spros pa'i gsung sgrog cing /'dus pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal ba/zhing bcu ru tra dar dus su/snying stobs brtson 'grus ngar

8b3(ya): chad pho mo'i thod pa la/sru gri sngags kyi 'khor lo bri//pho thod steng la mo thod 'og/rkang rjes sa dang dri ma dang /dug sna khrag sna thang phrom pa/grogs mkhar phungs phye dang bsre//dgra bo'i 'dra 'bag ling ga bya/ras sam gr-i ga dag la ni/ling ga
8b4(ya): bris la snying khar bzhugs/thod nang bsgyel la 'brub khung nang /gzung thag btags la lha dang dbye//oM ma hA kA la a mr-i shag phye shag phye//ma ma trag ru tra mA yA phaT/dme rjes dug gis bdugs byas nas/skam rlon thun rdzas zod par
8b5(ya): bcal/lug nag g.yag nag bya rog gsob/rgod dang bya rog rgod dang mnga'//'khor lo sta gzigs dgra snying bcas//rnal gsob skying dang sbal min can//gtor ma sha khrag chang gis bran//sku gsung thugs khros pa'i rgyus las/dkyi lor
8b6(ya): bsgrub rdzas bca' bzhi'i le'u ste dgu pa'o///yang ni bshad kyis legs nyon dang /pad+ma nyi zla'i gdan stengs na/rje dpon gsal bar sngags 'dis bong /oM ma ta ka la kha mu kha hU~M sa ma ya srog dung drung dza dzA/ma ma rag mo
8b7(ya): dz+wa li ru hring dza rag+ha ra hUM la sa hring dza/gdan drangs bzhugs bstod mchod gtor dbul/sang+yas bstan bsrung thu bo che/yab yul 'khor bcas dgongs shing gshegs/bdud kyi bar chad gsal ba dang /gdugs pa'o dgu bgegs bsgral
9a1(ya): ba'i phyir/drag po'i gzhal yas dkyir 'dir/gnyis med gdan bzhugs phris mdzod//sa ma ya a kaR+Sha ya hrI dza/a be shA ya tiSh+Tha lhan/khams gsum dregs pa'i mthu bo che/'jigs byed me 'bar ldag/
9a2(ya): yum sru//las mgon gang gi tshogs la phyag 'tshalo//bza' dang bca' dang btung ba dang /bshes rang zhal zas sna tshogs dang /sgra dang dri dang ro dang reg/bdud rtsi sha khrag gtor ma 'bul/dgyes par dgongs la bzhesu gsol//ma hA kA la NA ni
9a3(ya): ni sa ma ya/d+hu pe/puSh+pe/g+han d+he hUM/nai be oM lo shrap+ta hUM/ma hA kA la sa ma ya/sarba pu tsa ho//b+ha ling ta khA hi/ma hA ya mAM sA kha hi/ma hA tsit+ta khA hi/ma hA su ra khA hi/pra pe kar ma sarba an+da sid+hi ye swA hA/de ltar dus bzhi mchod cing 'od/sku

9a6(ya): bruM rbad song /nr-i ya nI ya tsa du na sod/a mo ka sa ka rbud mA ra ya srog la thun b+h+yo/dgra che ge mo mA ra ye rbang song song/ram ha de'i de ba byi nu tsan tra ru pa ni ra hu la dgra po'i snying la khrag rbadade/he ka laM ma hA tsan+d+ha mA ra ya rbad rbad/'bar ma seng gdong bza'
9a7(ya): bdud kyis/dgra bo gnas pa'i sa phyog su//kha ltas nga ro drag pos zang /bskul 'di btang zhing mi lpags bskul/hUM mi mngon dbyings na bzhugs pa yis/bstan bsrung dang ldan nag po che/rigs lnga khrag 'thung sprul pa ste/chos skyong mgon po maH/
9b1(ya): bo che ba bka' bsrung chen po dam tshig can//sku mdog mthing nag 'bar ba la/zhal cig phyag bzhi 'jigs pa'i gzugs//dbu skra kham nag gyen la brdzes/sbrul dang thod pa'i phreng ba bcings//zhal nas 'jigs pa'i khro gnyer gyis/bdud dpung ma
9b2(ya): lus skrag par mdzod//spyan gsum dmar la 'khrug pa yis/sdang mig phyogs bcu kun tu lta/shangs nas drag po'i 'thor rlung 'bud/s+phu ra ag tshoms smini ma ser/zhal gdangs ha ha hUM phaT sgrogs//mche gtsigs ljags 'dril rkan sgra

10a1(ya): /rdzas 'di bzhes la/dam nyams dgra bo'i snying khrag 'jigs/dus la bab po dza ti ma/he ru ka'i spyan snga ru/khas blangs yab yum da sdug cig/oM ma hA kA li dza ti ma hUM hUM/dgra bo'i bla rtsa la thu rim b+h+yo/hUM yis mgon po yab

10b2(ya): byas ta b+h+yo dpal ldan mgon po nag po yis//gsung gis las mkhan mthu rtsal can//khas blangs bzhin du dam las 'dang /rag khrag gi rba klong nas/'bar ma nag mo sku bskyod cig/zhal cig phya gnyis sku mdog nag/ral po kham nag mun ltar

10b4(ya): gcod la brtson/khyed kyis thugs dam rgyud bskul gyis/dmar gyi gtor ma 'di gsol la//dgra bo'i bla tshe long la shog/thun khrag phob la nad yams thong //bcol ba'i las rnams grub par mdzod//ma ma rag mo dza la dgra bo'i ni ma ra thu ril

25a1(ya): @/de yi steng du nyi ma la/gri gug yu bar hUM gnas pa/'od zer rnam pa gnyisu 'bros/steng dang 'og gi phyogs dag tu/rlung gis me sbar zod pa dros/gri gug gis ni dkrugs par bsam/dros pas dam tshig bdud rtsir gyur/steng gi
